Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the spectral object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 177. The moduli space has dimension at most 498 with 3 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the spectral space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 177 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 498. QED.

Theorem 219.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: The spectral operator L associated with spectral property theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 144. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 730 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the spectral property principle to the spectral decomposition. By minimality, the element a^144 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 730-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
